Note on `scripts/`: a plainly named root folder (no dot-folders, owner, September 26) for owner-run Node scripts; core owns only `scripts/README.md`, alerts owns `scripts/register-x-webhook.mjs`; no folder is owned whole. Header and footer: core creates `components/site-header.tsx` and `components/site-footer.tsx` as new files (S19); landing later switches its imports to them and deletes its own `landing-header.tsx` and `landing-footer.tsx`; no file has two writers.

## Premises

The owner's words, taken as given (all September 28 unless dated): planning is over and one feature builds the whole product; three tiers (Hobby $5 / 100 posts / daily DM, Creator $30 / 3,000 / daily, Wire $99 / 4,000 / every 15 minutes), sites and feeds unlimited; the free week starts at the first outside view, 300 watched posts, 14-day pause, day-seven freeze with prices, checkout as sign-up, no card before; $200 total for builds without sign-up, $3 reserved per build; sites and feeds every minute, X accounts every minute for Wire and every 5 otherwise; one DM per story ever with the story link, no instant alerts; watched X posts treated like articles with the account shown; the brief on the person saved once and read by Jev and the writer; the profile request trimmed; the person-facing name "agent"; grouping in this build (72h/0.75/0.75 assistant defaults), fit 0.5/0.35, Qwen 3.7 Flash writer with Jev checks, day zero 10 per source from 2 days; spending guards in code; four Slack alerts for the test period; the source watchdog at $1 a day; the bot check passed (a real DM sent with the stored bot token; @oparax_ai user id 2098571482792112140); Contact stores and emails no-reply@oparax.ai; the owner and Kush start from the blank page; star thresholds in; the design source is stock shadcn Mira per DESIGN.md; no test suite; the build pauses only for money, an owner ruling, deleting live data, real DMs or charges, and DESIGN.md.

Design source: stock shadcn Mira per DESIGN.md.

Plan files: `.feature/plan-<N>.md` (this file), `.feature/plan-<N>/shared.md`, `.feature/plan-<N>/<id>.md` for each row above, `.feature/plan-<N>-owner.md` (the approved plain plan).

-----
# .feature/plan-draft/shared.md
# Shared contracts for plan <N>

Every slice cites these instead of restating them. A number here carries its attribution; a number that appears in a slice without a citation is a defect. Costs come from `docs/references/cogs.md` (unit prices: X post read or delivered $0.005, user lookup $0.010, counts request $0.005, DM send $0.015, DM received $0.010; Qwen 3.7 Flash $0.03 in / $0.13 out per million; Jev $0.042 per million input; GPT-6 Luna fast's price is read per call from the Gateway response).

## 1. Table shapes (built by data-model; the only SQL in the plan)

All tables in `public`, RLS enabled on every one, deny-all by default (no policy for `anon`; engines and pages read and write through the admin client, server-only). The only policies: SELECT for `authenticated` on `monitors` where `(select auth.uid()) = user_id`, and on `monitor_sources`, `monitor_accounts`, `followed_repos` where the row's `monitor_id` belongs to such a monitor. No INSERT, UPDATE or DELETE policy anywhere: every mutation goes through a server action that proves ownership with the RLS-scoped client and then writes with the admin client (Astra, turn 1: an owner UPDATE policy would let a payer change `tier` or `paid_through` through the Data API). `citext` already exists in the project. Timestamps are `timestamptz`, default `now()` where named `created_at`/`updated_at`.

- `sources`: `id text primary key` (the seed's id, or `x-<handle lowercase>` for an account added later, or `web-<host>-<sha1 of url, 8 chars>` for a site added later), `kind text not null check (kind in ('rss','website','x_account'))`, `target text not null`, `name text not null`, `focus text not null default ''`, `lang text not null default 'en'`, `description text not null default ''`, `items_per_week numeric`, `posts_per_day numeric`, `followers integer`, `recent_titles jsonb not null default '[]'`, `x_user_id text`, `x_since_id text` (the newest post id polled for an x_account source, shared by every watcher), `etag text`, `last_modified text`, `last_fetched_at timestamptz`, `next_fetch_at timestamptz not null default now()`, `last_item_at timestamptz`, `unreadable_streak integer not null default 0`, `paused_at timestamptz`, `paused_reason text`, `created_at`, `updated_at`. Index `(next_fetch_at) where paused_at is null`. Seeded from `docs/source-table-seed.json` (150 rows) by the migration (`insert ... on conflict (id) do update` on the descriptive columns only).
- `monitors`: `id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid()`, `handle citext not null unique`, `display_handle text not null`, `x_user_id text`, `beat text not null`, `profile jsonb` (`{name, bio, image, site}`), `build_state jsonb` (the engine's checkpoints for resume: `{profile, posts, scores, answer, searched}`, read by nothing else), `brief jsonb` (`{summary: string, interests: string[], languages: string[], topic_terms: string[]}`, written in the same Luna call as the picks), `status text not null check (status in ('building','failed','live','paused','frozen'))`, `build_step integer not null default 0`, `build_log jsonb not null default '[]'` (array of `{step, message, at}`), `build_error text`, `build_tries integer not null default 0`, `build_lease_until timestamptz`, `build_started_at`, `build_finished_at`, `built_by uuid references auth.users(id)`, `user_id uuid references auth.users(id)`, `tier text not null default 'free' check (tier in ('free','hobby','creator','wire'))`, `trial_started_at timestamptz`, `last_viewed_at timestamptz`, `paid_through timestamptz`, `stripe_customer_id text`, `stripe_subscription_id text`, `subscription_status text`, `cancel_at_period_end boolean not null default false`, `last_invoice_id text`, `checkout_session_id text`, `checkout_opened_at timestamptz`, `pool_limit integer not null default 300`, `pool_used integer not null default 0`, `pool_period_start timestamptz`, `cadence text not null default 'daily' check (cadence in ('daily','every_15m'))`, `alert_hour integer not null default 14 check (alert_hour between 0 and 23)`, `alert_timezone text` (IANA name; null means UTC), `last_alert_at timestamptz`, `bot_x_user_id text`, `bot_state text not null default 'none' check (bot_state in ('none','active','paused','stopped'))`, `bot_code text`, `bot_code_expires_at timestamptz`, `bot_connected_at timestamptz`, `digest_github boolean not null default false`, `digest_product_hunt boolean not null default false`, `created_at`, `updated_at`. Indexes: `(status)`, `(stripe_customer_id)`, unique `(user_id) where user_id is not null` (one agent per account; Astra, turn 1), unique `(x_user_id) where x_user_id is not null`.
- `monitor_sources`: `monitor_id uuid references monitors(id) on delete cascade`, `source_id text references sources(id)`, `score numeric`, `why text not null default ''`, `added_by text not null check (added_by in ('onboarding','person'))`, `no_filter boolean not null default false`, `prefilled_at timestamptz` (null until day zero ran for this pair; a source added later is prefilled the same way), `removed_at timestamptz`, `created_at`; primary key `(monitor_id, source_id)`.
- `monitor_accounts`: `monitor_id uuid references monitors(id) on delete cascade`, `handle citext not null`, `x_user_id text`, `name text not null default ''`, `score numeric`, `why text not null default ''`, `watched boolean not null default false`, `posts_per_day numeric`, `counts_checked_at timestamptz`, `created_at`; primary key `(monitor_id, handle)`.
- `items`: `id text primary key` (articles: `sha1` hex of the canonical final URL per R1; posts: `x:<post id>`), `source_id text not null references sources(id)` (the source it was first found through), `source_ids text[] not null default '{}'` (every source it was found through, R1; appended on a repeat sighting), `kind text not null check (kind in ('article','post'))`, `url text not null`, `final_url text`, `title text not null default ''`, `text text not null default ''`, `published_at timestamptz not null`, `date_source text`, `text_from text`, `outcome text not null check (outcome in ('full','short','unreadable'))`, `unreadable_reason text`, `image text`, `lang text`, `author jsonb` (posts: `{handle, name, x_user_id}`), `created_at`. Index `(source_id, published_at desc)`.
- `monitor_items`: `monitor_id uuid`, `item_id text references items(id)`, `status text not null check (status in ('pending','skipped','on','attached','storied','failed'))`, `fit_score numeric`, `fit_band text check (fit_band in ('on','off','unsure'))`, `story_id uuid`, `card jsonb` (the single-source card, verified shape below), `card_status text check (card_status in ('written','no_card','write_failed'))`, `error text`, `tries integer not null default 0`, `judged_at timestamptz`, `created_at`; primary key `(monitor_id, item_id)`; index `(monitor_id, status)`.
- `stories`: `id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid()`, `monitor_id uuid references monitors(id) on delete cascade`, `opened_at timestamptz not null`, `last_published_at timestamptz not null`, `last_changed_at timestamptz not null`, `headline text`, `card jsonb` (verified card), `fallback_title text not null`, `image text`, `status text not null check (status in ('open','written','no_card','write_failed'))`, `version integer not null default 0`, `alerted_at timestamptz`, `created_at`. Index `(monitor_id, last_published_at desc)`, `(monitor_id, alerted_at) where alerted_at is null`.
- `story_items`: `story_id uuid references stories(id) on delete cascade`, `item_id text references items(id)`, `role text not null check (role in ('opened','joined','attached'))`, `join_score numeric`, `adds_score numeric`, `added_at timestamptz not null default now()`; primary key `(story_id, item_id)`.
- `card_versions`: `id bigint generated always as identity primary key`, `story_id uuid references stories(id) on delete cascade`, `version integer not null`, `card jsonb`, `record jsonb not null` (the write record: prompts, attempts, drops, scores, repair, `headline_from`, `final`), `created_at`; unique `(story_id, version)`.
- `deliveries`: `monitor_id uuid`, `story_id uuid references stories(id) on delete cascade`, `state text not null check (state in ('sending','sent','failed','held'))`, `dm_event_id text`, `attempted_at timestamptz not null default now()`, `sent_at timestamptz`, `tries integer not null default 1`, `error text`; primary key `(monitor_id, story_id)`; index `(sent_at)`.
- `dm_events`: `event_id text primary key`, `monitor_id uuid`, `sender_x_user_id text`, `text text`, `received_at timestamptz not null default now()`, `handled text` (`activated`, `stop`, `pause`, `resume`, `ignored`, `logged`), `payload jsonb`.
- `stripe_events`: `id text primary key`, `type text not null`, `received_at timestamptz not null default now()`, `processed_at timestamptz`, `error text`.
- `cost_ledger`: `id bigint generated always as identity primary key`, `service text not null check (service in ('x','gateway','jev','reservation','stripe'))`, `kind text not null`, `units integer not null default 1`, `usd numeric(10,6) not null` (the settled amount; equals `usd_reserved` until settled), `usd_reserved numeric(10,6) not null default 0` (the upper bound written before the call), `settled boolean not null default false`, `monitor_id uuid`, `source_id text`, `run_id text`, `external_id text`, `day date not null default (now() at time zone 'utc')::date`, `hour timestamptz not null default date_trunc('hour', now())`, `created_at`; unique index `(service, external_id, day) where external_id is not null`; index `(day)`, `(monitor_id, day)`, `(source_id, day)`.
- `run_claims`: `job text primary key`, `run_id text not null`, `claimed_at timestamptz not null`, `expires_at timestamptz not null`.
- `config`: `key text primary key`, `value jsonb not null`, `updated_at`. Keys seeded: `kill_switch` `false`; `anon_budget_usd` `200`; `x_balance` `{"console": null, "checked_at": null, "status": "unknown"}`; `bot` `{"x_user_id": null, "handle": null}`; `stripe_prices` `{}`.
- `contact_messages`: `id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid()`, `email text not null`, `message text not null`, `created_at`, `emailed_at timestamptz`, `error text`.
- `handle_waitlist`: `id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid()`, `handle citext not null`, `beat text`, `created_at`.
- `digest_items`: `id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid()`, `monitor_id uuid references monitors(id) on delete cascade`, `kind text not null check (kind in ('github_repo','product_hunt','star_threshold'))`, `external_id text not null`, `name text not null`, `url text not null`, `why_now text not null`, `description text not null default ''`, `fit_score numeric`, `created_at`; unique `(monitor_id, kind, external_id)`.
- `followed_repos`: `monitor_id uuid references monitors(id) on delete cascade`, `repo text not null` (`owner/name`), `threshold integer not null default 3000`, `stars integer`, `crossed_at timestamptz`, `checked_at timestamptz`, `created_at`; primary key `(monitor_id, repo)`.

RPCs (all `security definer`, `set search_path = public`, `revoke execute from public, anon, authenticated` so only the service role calls them; the admin client calls `.rpc(name, args)`):

- `reserve_build(p_monitor uuid, p_usd numeric) returns boolean`: in one statement, sums `cost_ledger` rows with `service = 'reservation'` and, if that sum plus `p_usd` is at most `config.anon_budget_usd`, inserts the reservation row (`kind = 'build'`, `monitor_id = p_monitor`, `external_id = p_monitor::text`) and returns true; else false. Locks with `select ... for update` on the config row.
- `debit_posts(p_monitor uuid, p_item_ids text[]) returns table (attached text[], remaining integer, paused boolean)`: in one transaction, locks the monitor row, computes `room = pool_limit - pool_used`, inserts `monitor_items (monitor_id, item_id, status = 'pending')` for the first `room` ids not already present (ordered as given, oldest first), increments `pool_used` by the number actually inserted, and returns the inserted ids, the remaining room and `pool_used >= pool_limit`. A post already attached is never debited twice (the primary key), and the pool can never exceed its limit (Astra, turn 1).
- `claim_run(p_job text, p_run_id text, p_ttl_seconds integer) returns boolean`: inserts or updates `run_claims` only when no unexpired claim exists for `p_job`; returns whether the claim was taken. `p_ttl_seconds` is the route's `maxDuration` plus 60 (the lease outlives the function).
- `release_run(p_job text, p_run_id text) returns void`: deletes the claim if it belongs to `p_run_id`.
- `claim_build(p_monitor uuid, p_lease_seconds integer) returns boolean`: takes or renews `build_lease_until` on a `building` monitor whose lease is null or expired and whose `build_tries < 2`, incrementing `build_tries` when taking a fresh lease; returns whether the caller may run the build.
- `user_id_by_email(p_email text) returns uuid`: `select id from auth.users where lower(email) = lower(p_email) limit 1`.

## 2. The cost ledger (owned by core; every paid caller writes through it)

`reserveCost({service, kind, usdReserved, monitorId?, sourceId?, runId?}): Promise<{ id }>` and `settleCost(id, { usd, units?, externalId? })` (seam S1): before every paid call one row is inserted with the upper bound (`usd_reserved`, `settled = false`); after the response it is settled in place with the real amount (a duplicate `(service, external_id, day)` on settle means X already billed that id today: the row is settled at $0). A reservation that cannot be written is a failure of the call (fail closed: nothing paid runs without its row; Astra, turn 1). An unsettled row older than 10 minutes counts as spent at its reserved amount in every sum. Upper bounds (assistant's defaults): one X post read $0.005 per post that `max_results` allows, one user lookup $0.010, one counts request $0.005, one DM send $0.015, one Luna fast call $0.50, one Qwen call $0.02, one Jev request $0.01. PostHog receives one `spend_recorded` event per settled row (properties: service, kind, usd, monitor_id, source_id).

| Paid call | service | kind | unit price (cogs.md) | who pays |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| X user lookup | x | user | $0.010 | the page's reservation (trial) or the tier |
| X post read (timeline, search, tweets) | x | post | $0.005 per returned post id, once per UTC day | same |
| X counts request | x | counts | $0.005 | the tier (picker) |
| X DM send | x | dm_send | $0.015 | same |
| X DM received (webhook event) | x | dm_received | $0.010 | same |
| Gateway model call (Luna fast, Qwen) | gateway | the stage name (`onboarding`, `brief`, `write`, `repair`, `topics`) | read from `providerMetadata.gateway.cost` (fallback `marketCost`) | same |
| Jev request | jev | the question set (`rank`, `fit`, `group`, `adds`, `support`, `headline`, `digest`) | read from the evaluate response's `providerMetadata` | same |
| Anonymous build | reservation | build | $3.00 (assistant's arithmetic in the owner plan) | the $200 total; every build without a signed-in payer, the owner's included (no special seats: owner, September 28) |
| X DM received (webhook) | x | dm_received | $0.010, recorded on receipt (no reservation is possible for an event X initiates) | the tier or the reservation |

Caps and floors (all read at the top of every paid path, seam S2 `guards()`):
- Kill switch: `config.kill_switch = true` stops every paid path and every cron run (they exit with `run_skipped` and a PostHog event `run_skipped` reason `kill_switch`).
- Anonymous builds: `reserve_build` against `config.anon_budget_usd` = 200 (owner, September 28: "$200 total"); reservation $3 per build. A refused reservation is `build_refused` (PostHog) and the box shows the copy in section 5.
- X credit floor: the estimate (`config.x_balance.console` minus the ledger's X spend since `checked_at`) under $15 (owner's ruling, the number the assistant's inside it) pauses anonymous builds and trial-tier watched-account polling; paid tiers continue. A balance never read, or last read more than 25 hours ago, counts as unknown and closes the same two things (the owner plan: "or the daily balance read fails"); `guards()` then triggers one read on the spot (an unpriced metadata call) before answering.
- Daily credits read: `GET /2/usage/credits` once a day (core's cron) writes `config.x_balance.console = data.total_balance`, `checked_at`, `status = 'ok'`; a failed read writes `status = 'failed'` and keeps the last value.
- Source watchdog: a source whose `cost_ledger` rows (reserved unsettled included) sum to more than $1.00 on the current UTC day (owner, September 28, accepting the assistant's rule) gets `paused_at = now()`, `paused_reason = 'spend'`; PostHog `source_paused`. The feed cron applies it before processing each source's items (so a runaway stops within the minute; Astra, turn 1: daily is too late) and core's daily cron re-checks every source. No Slack alert of its own (the owner's four only).
- Tries: any model or X call fails after the Gateway's or the client's own single retry → the work item is marked `failed` with the error after 2 tries in total (`tries` column), never a third.

## 3. Clock, pool and cadence rules (owned by core's `monitorState`, seam S3)

`monitorState(m: MonitorRow, now: Date)` returns one of: `building`, `failed`, `dormant` (built, no outside view yet, less than 14 days since `build_finished_at`), `paused` (14 days or more since the later of `build_finished_at` and `last_viewed_at`; owner, September 28: a page nobody views), `trial` (with `daysLeft`, `trialEndsAt = trial_started_at + 7 days`), `frozen` (trial over, `paid_through` null or past), `paid` (`paid_through > now`), `lapsed` (`paid_through` passed after a paid period; treated as frozen with the renewal copy). Rules every scheduler and sender apply:

| Work | dormant | trial | paid | paused, frozen, lapsed, building, failed |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| collect (sites and feeds) and feed (judge, group, write) | runs (owner: "the feed runs from build") | runs | runs | no |
| watched X accounts polling | no (nothing spent before a view) | runs, pool 300, from `trial_started_at` onward (no backfill; Astra, turn 1) | runs, pool by tier | no |
| bot sends | no | runs | runs | no |
| digests | no | no (switches need an account) | runs when switched on | no |

Pool: `pool_limit` 300 in trial; 100 / 3,000 / 4,000 by tier after payment (owner, September 28); `pool_used` reset to 0 and `pool_period_start = now()` on every `invoice.paid`. At zero the accounts pause (no polling) until the reset; sites continue. Cadence: `daily` for free, hobby, creator; `every_15m` for wire; the daily slot is `alert_hour` (default 14) in `alert_timezone` (set from the `x-vercel-ip-timezone` header at the first outside view; null → UTC). The free week's first outside view: `trial_started_at` set once, `last_viewed_at` on every view, by the beacon route (page component) after the bot check and the owner exclusion (`OWNER_EMAILS`). X polling cadence: every minute for wire, every 5 minutes otherwise (owner, September 28; the app limit is 10,000 requests per 15 minutes). Sites and feeds: every minute (owner, September 28). X DM limits (docs.x.com): 1,440 sends per app per day, 15 per recipient per 15 minutes.

## 4. Ids: routes, jobs, events, env

Routes (public unless noted):
- `/` landing; `/<handle>` the agent page; `/<handle>/<storyId>` the page with that story in view; `/<handle>/settings` (signed-in owner of the page only); `/login`, `/signup`, `/forgot-password`, `/auth/confirm`, `/auth/reset-password`; `/checkout/return?session_id=` ; `/privacy`, `/terms`.
- `POST /api/build` (form post: `handle`, `beat`; runs the X lookup before answering so `Handle not found` shows under the box (owner, September 27); redirects 303 to `/<handle>`, or to `/?closed=1` when refused, or to `/?handle=&error=handle|beat|notfound|lookup` on validation); `POST /api/build/retry` (json `{monitorId}`); `POST /api/view` (json `{monitorId}`, bot-checked); `POST /api/activation` (form post `monitorId`; issues the one-time code and redirects to the X composer); `POST /api/stripe/checkout` (json `{monitorId, tier}` → `{url}`); `POST /api/stripe/webhook` (raw body); `POST /api/stripe/portal` (signed-in owner → `{url}`); `GET|POST /api/x/webhook` (CRC and events); `POST /api/contact` (json `{email, message}`); crons under `/api/cron/*` below.
- Reserved handles (refused by the build route and never routed as pages): `login, signup, forgot-password, auth, checkout, privacy, terms, api, settings, onboarding, opengraph-image, favicon.ico, icon.svg, apple-icon.png, robots.txt, sitemap.xml, _next, static, public, admin, oparax, oparax_ai`; kept in `lib/x/handle.ts` as `RESERVED_HANDLES` (core).

Cron jobs (declared once in `vercel.json` by core; each route checks `Authorization: Bearer ${CRON_SECRET}`, takes `claim_run(job, runId, ttl)` and releases it; every route exports `maxDuration = 300` except where noted):

| path | schedule | owner | job name | ttl |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `/api/cron/collect` | `* * * * *` | collect | `collect` | 360 s |
| `/api/cron/feed` | `* * * * *` | feed | `feed` | 360 s |
| `/api/cron/accounts` | `* * * * *` | accounts | `accounts` | 360 s |
| `/api/cron/alerts` | `* * * * *` | alerts | `alerts` | 360 s |
| `/api/cron/digests` | `0 6 * * *` | digests | `digests` | 860 s (maxDuration 800) |
| `/api/cron/credits` | `0 5 * * *` | core | `credits` | 360 s |
| `/api/cron/builds` | `*/5 * * * *` | onboarding | `builds` | 360 s |
| `/api/cron/contact` | `0 * * * *` | landing | `contact` | 360 s |

Every route exports `maxDuration = 300` (digests 800) and stops admitting new work after 240 seconds (700 for digests; assistant's defaults) so it ends before the platform kills it. Every cron run writes PostHog `run_completed` (properties: job, run_id, counts) or `run_failed` (job, run_id, error); a skipped claim writes nothing (another run holds it).

PostHog events (one per intent; `snake_case`, past tense; server-side through `lib/analytics/events.ts` seam S4 `track(event, props, distinctId?)`; browser-side only where named): `agent_build_requested` (browser, the box), `agent_build_started`, `agent_built`, `agent_build_failed`, `build_refused` (reason: `budget`, `credits`, `kill_switch`, `bot`, `reserved_handle`, `duplicate`), `trial_started`, `page_viewed_outside` (first view only), `bot_connected`, `bot_paused`, `bot_stopped`, `story_alerted`, `delivery_held`, `paywall_shown` (browser), `checkout_started`, `payment_succeeded`, `payment_failed`, `subscription_cancelled`, `signin_link_sent`, `source_added`, `source_removed`, `source_no_filter_changed`, `account_watch_changed`, `alert_hour_changed`, `digest_switched`, `repo_followed`, `contact_sent`, `spend_recorded`, `source_paused`, `run_completed`, `run_failed`, `run_skipped`. The four Slack alerts at ship (owner): daily sum of `spend_recorded.usd` where service = x over $2; `config.x_balance.console` under $20 (emitted as `x_balance_read` daily with `usd`); no `run_completed` with job `collect` in an hour; any `build_refused`.

Environment (names; values never in git): existing `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`, `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY`, `X_BEARER_TOKEN` (app read), `X_BOT_BEARER_TOKEN` (the bot's own token; sends DMs; proven September 28), `X_CLIENT_SECRET` (signs the Activity API CRC and events), `AI_GATEWAY_API_KEY`, `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`, `STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_PROJECT_TOKEN`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_HOST`, `GITHUB_TOKEN`, `PRODUCT_HUNT_TOKEN`. New, owner adds at ship (each slice's part 4 repeats only its own): `CRON_SECRET`, `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `OWNER_EMAILS` (comma-separated), `SMTP_USER`, `SMTP_PASSWORD`. Code reads `x-vercel-ip-timezone` for the timezone.

## 5. Copy (every shared user-facing string; a component's private strings live in its own content module)

- Handle not found: `Handle not found` (owner, September 27).
- Box closed: `Builds are unavailable right now. You can leave your handle.` Button `Leave my handle`. Saved: `Your handle is saved.`
- Lookup failed: `The X lookup could not finish. Try again.`
- Building steps (the page's chain of steps): `Looking up @{handle} on X`, `Reading @{handle}'s newest posts`, `Choosing sources and X accounts`, `Writing the brief`.
- Build failed: `Building stopped at: {step}. {reason}` Button `Try again`.
- Trial line: `{n} days left in your free week. Plans from $5 a month.` (n from `monitorState`).
- Frozen banner title: `Your free week is over.` Body: `Your agent has stopped watching. Old cards stay readable. Pick a plan to keep it running.` Buttons: `Hobby, $5 a month`, `Creator, $30 a month`, `Wire, $99 a month`. Tier lines under each: `100 watched X posts a month, one DM a day` / `3,000 watched X posts a month, one DM a day` / `4,000 watched X posts a month, a digest every 15 minutes`. Everywhere: `Sites and feeds unlimited.`
- Lapsed banner: `Payment failed. Update your card to keep your agent running.` Button `Update card`.
- Paused (dormant 14 days): `This agent paused because nobody visited it. It is starting again now.`
- Pool: `{used} of {limit} watched posts used this month.` At zero: `Your watched X accounts are paused until {reset date}. Sites and feeds keep running.`
- Source health: `Could not read its last {n} items.`
- Skipped item: `{score} against your beat`.
- Unverified report: `Unverified report` (an item with no surviving fact), with its link and language tag.
- Further reports on a story: `{n} further reports`.
- Bot button: `Get alerts on X`; helper: `Opens X with a one-time code addressed to @oparax_ai. Send it from @{handle} to connect.` Connected: `Alerts on. Reply STOP to the bot to stop.`
- DM text (one DM, up to 10 stories, assistant's default): first line `Oparax: {n} new {story|stories} for you`, then per story `{headline}\n{first fact}\n{link}` separated by a blank line. Link: `https://oparax.ai/{handle}/{storyId}`.
- The bot sends no replies and no acknowledgements (owner: the bot carries news only); the page shows the state: `Alerts on.`, `Alerts paused. Reply RESUME to the bot.`, `Alerts stopped. Connect again to get news.`; a code from another X account is ignored (owner plan: "a message from any other account is ignored").
- Checkout return: `You are set. We emailed a sign-in link to {email}. Your agent is running again.` Pending: `Payment received. We are connecting your account; check {email} in a minute.`
- Wrong-account settings: `This page belongs to the account that paid for it. Sign in with that email.`
- No-agent sign-in: the landing box with the line `You have no agent yet. Type your handle and beat to build one.`
- Contact: title `Contact Us`, fields `Email`, `Message`, button `Send`, done `Thanks, we got your message.` (never claims an email was sent), mail error hidden from the person (stored, `error` column).
- Agent naming: every heading uses `agent` (`Your Oparax agent`, `@{handle}'s agent`).

## 6. Integration seams (owner, callers, exact shape)

- S1 `reserveCost(input: ReserveInput): Promise<{ id: number }>` and `settleCost(id: number, actual: { usd: number; units?: number; externalId?: string }): Promise<void>` in `lib/guards/ledger.ts` (core); `ReserveInput = { service: 'x'|'gateway'|'jev'|'reservation'; kind: string; usdReserved: number; monitorId?: string; sourceId?: string; runId?: string }`. A helper `withCost(input, fn)` reserves, runs `fn`, settles with what `fn` returns (`{ usd, units?, externalId? }`), and on a thrown error settles at the reserved amount with `kind + ':failed'`. Callers: core's X and Jev clients (internal), onboarding, feed and digests (Gateway calls through S7's `withCost`), alerts (through the X client). The daily reconcile settles rows left unsettled for over 10 minutes at their reserved amount.
- S2 `guards(): Promise<{ killSwitch: boolean; anonBuildsOpen: boolean; trialPollingOpen: boolean; balanceEstimate: number | null }>` in `lib/guards/guards.ts` (core). Read at the top of every paid path and every cron. Callers: onboarding (build route), accounts, alerts, feed, collect, digests, page (to show the closed box state on redirect).
- S3 `monitorState(m: Pick<MonitorRow, 'status' | 'build_finished_at' | 'trial_started_at' | 'last_viewed_at' | 'paid_through' | 'tier' | 'pool_limit' | 'pool_used' | 'cadence' | 'subscription_status'>, now?: Date): { state: 'building'|'failed'|'dormant'|'paused'|'trial'|'frozen'|'paid'|'lapsed'; daysLeft: number|null; trialEndsAt: Date|null; poolOpen: boolean; cadence: 'daily'|'every_15m' }` in `lib/monitor-state.ts` (core). Callers: page, settings, collect, feed, accounts, alerts, digests, access.
- S4 `track(event: EventName, props: Record<string, unknown>, distinctId?: string): void` and the `EventName` union in `lib/analytics/events.ts` (core; server-only, via the PostHog Node client with `waitUntil`). Browser events use `posthog.capture` directly with the same names.
- S5 X client in `lib/x/client.ts` (core): `xGet(path, params, opts: { token?: 'app'|'bot'; monitorId?; sourceId?; runId?; maxPosts: number })`, `xPost(path, body, opts)`; the engine's one-request-per-second queue (per instance; the app-wide 10,000 per 15 minutes is respected by the cadences, not by this queue) and one retry on 429 honoring `x-rate-limit-reset`; reserves through S1 before the call (`maxPosts × $0.005` plus `$0.010` per expected profile) and settles per returned post and user id (the engine's `xBill` moved here) and per DM send; `xCounts(handle): Promise<number | null>` (posts per day over 7 days, `-is:reply -is:retweet`, one counts request; null on failure, never zero); `xUserByHandle(handle)`; `xTimeline(userId, { sinceId?, startTime?, maxResults })` with `exclude=replies,retweets`; `xSendDm(recipientXUserId, text): Promise<{ ok: true; dmEventId: string } | { ok: false; status: number; error: string; uncertain: boolean }>` (uncertain = timeout or 5xx with no body). Callers: onboarding, accounts, alerts, settings, core's credits cron.
- S6 Jev client in `lib/ai/jev.ts` (core): `jev<T extends Record<string, Question>>(state: object, questions: T, opts: { kind: string; monitorId?; sourceId? }): Promise<Record<keyof T, number>>` over `https://ai-gateway.vercel.sh/v1/evaluate` with `typesafe-ai/jev` and the request shape `engine.ts` uses today (`type: 'boolean'` questions, `answers.<id>.probability`; proven September 27), schema-checked, one retry, bills through S1. Batches at 60 questions per request (R10); a state that with one question still exceeds the documented 32,000-token state limit makes the call fail with `state_too_large` (never truncated). Callers: onboarding, feed, digests.
- S7 `gatewayCost(providerMetadata): { charged: number; market: number }` in `lib/ai/cost.ts` (core): `charged` is `gateway.cost` as returned (zero is zero), `market` is `gateway.marketCost`; the ledger records `charged` (the old `cost || marketCost` fallback in `engine.ts` is removed; Astra, turn 1). Callers: onboarding, feed, digests.
- S8 `claimRun(job, ttlSeconds): Promise<{ runId: string; release: () => Promise<void> } | null>` in `lib/guards/claims.ts` (core), over the RPCs. Callers: every cron route.
- S9 The build route contract (onboarding): `POST /api/build` form fields `handle`, `beat`; responses as in section 4. The landing form (landing) posts here; the no-agent sign-in state (access) renders the same form component from landing (`components/landing/build-box.tsx`, exported `BuildBox({ closed?: boolean; error?: string; handle?: string })`).
- S10 The onboarding output rows (onboarding writes, page and settings and accounts and feed read): `monitors.profile`, `monitors.brief`, `monitors.status = 'live'`, `monitors.build_finished_at`, `monitor_sources` rows (`added_by = 'onboarding'`), `monitor_accounts` rows (`watched = true` for every recommended account: owner, the free week watches the recommended accounts), `sources` rows upserted for recommended accounts that are not seed rows (`id = x-<handle>`, `kind = x_account`, `target = https://x.com/<handle>`).
- S11 Items and verdicts: collect writes `items` (articles) and inserts `monitor_items (status = 'pending')` for every monitor watching the source (`monitor_sources.removed_at is null`) in the same run; accounts writes `items` (posts) and attaches them per watching monitor through `debit_posts` (the pool); feed reads `monitor_items` and writes verdicts, `stories`, `story_items`, `card_versions`. A source added to a monitor later gets its day-zero prefill (`monitor_sources.prefilled_at` null) from the items already cached plus a fresh fetch.
- S12 The verified card shape (feed writes; page, alerts and landing read): `{ headline: string; facts: { text: string; support: number; attribution: number; evidence: { item: string; span: string }[] }[]; publishers: { source_id: string; name: string; url: string }[]; image: string | null; headline_from: 'writer'|'title'|'first fact' }`.
- S13 The view beacon (page owns the component and the route): `POST /api/view` `{ monitorId }` → sets `trial_started_at` once, `last_viewed_at`, `alert_timezone` if null (from `x-vercel-ip-timezone`; absent locally → UTC), restarts a paused monitor; excluded when the session user's email is in `OWNER_EMAILS` (owner: "his signed-in session is excluded; no IP lists"); bot-checked; a failed bot check does nothing. Emits `trial_started` and `page_viewed_outside` once. Localhost cannot prove Vercel's bot check or timezone header: the owner's at-ship walk on the deployed site is that proof (page slice, part 4).
- S14 Checkout (access owns; page calls): the freeze banner's three buttons POST `/api/stripe/checkout` with `{ monitorId, tier }` and follow `url`; the route refuses any state but `frozen` or `lapsed` (no card before day seven, owner) and reuses an open session (`checkout_session_id`, opened under 30 minutes ago) instead of creating a second. The lapsed banner's `Update card` POSTs `/api/stripe/portal` (signed-in) or, signed out, links to `/login?next=/<handle>`.
- S15 The activation code (page issues, alerts consumes): the bot button is a form posting to `POST /api/activation` (page's route) which writes a fresh 10-character random code (letters and digits) to `monitors.bot_code` with `bot_code_expires_at = now() + 30 minutes` (assistant's default) and redirects (303) to `https://x.com/messages/compose?recipient_id={config.bot.x_user_id}&text={code}`; a second press within the window reuses the code. Alerts consumes it once (sets `bot_code = null` on bind) and ignores an expired one. `config.bot` is filled by core's credits cron (and on first need) from `GET /2/users/me` with the bot token.
- S16 The single-source card: feed writes `monitor_items.card` in the S12 shape (publishers has one entry) for every item with `fit_band = 'on'`; the story card is separate; the page shows both views.
- S17 Digest rows (`digest_items`, `followed_repos`): digests writes, page reads, settings writes `followed_repos` and the two switches.
- S18 `scripts/register-x-webhook.mjs` (alerts owns the file; `scripts/README.md` is core's): registers `https://oparax.ai/api/x/webhook` with the app bearer (`POST /2/webhooks`) and creates the bot account's subscription with the bot token (`POST /2/activity/subscriptions`, docs.x.com/x-api/activity/create-x-activity-subscription; the exact body is a named build-time check against that page); run by the owner at ship; prints the webhook id and never a secret; a refusal is printed as the blocker it is.
- S19 Header and footer: core creates `components/site-header.tsx` (`SiteHeader({ signedIn })`) and `components/site-footer.tsx` (`SiteFooter()`) as new files with the content of today's `landing-header.tsx` and `landing-footer.tsx`; the footer's Contact trigger imports `ContactDialog` from `components/landing/contact-dialog.tsx` (landing owns; its props stay `{ triggerClassName, label? }`, and the dialog's new email field changes nothing in that signature). Landing switches its own page to the new files and deletes its two old ones in its own step. Callers: page, settings, access, landing.
- S20 Login copy: `lib/auth/content.ts` (access) holds every auth string; access's sign-out button copy moves there too; `landingContent.auth` is deleted by landing (which depends on access) in its own step. A signed-in user with no agent is redirected by access to `/?noagent=1`; landing renders the box with the no-agent line (no import across the two).
- S21 `StoryCard({ card, story, handle, compact?: boolean })` in `components/monitor/story-card.tsx` (page); landing imports it for the example agent's three latest stories.
- S22b Source discovery for the settings editor: `discoverChangeDetection(inputUrl: URL)` exported by `lib/sources/discovery.ts` (collect owns; existing signature at line 700 kept unchanged; settings calls it read-only).
- S22 Reserved handles and validation: `normalizeValidHandle`, `RESERVED_HANDLES`, `isReservedHandle(handle)` in `lib/x/handle.ts` (core). Callers: onboarding, page, landing.
- S23 Mail: `sendMail({ to, subject, text }): Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>` in `lib/contact/mail.ts` (landing) over `nodemailer` with `smtp.gmail.com:465` and `SMTP_USER`/`SMTP_PASSWORD`; only Contact calls it.
- S24 Stripe prices: `priceIdFor(tier): Promise<string>` in `lib/billing/prices.ts` (access) creates or finds by `lookup_key` (`oparax_hobby_monthly`, `oparax_creator_monthly`, `oparax_wire_monthly`; amounts 500, 3000, 9900 cents, `recurring.interval = month`, with a Stripe idempotency key per lookup key) and caches ids in `config.stripe_prices`.
- S25 The Activity API event shapes (alerts): CRC `GET ?crc_token=` → `{ response_token: 'sha256=' + base64(hmac_sha256(X_CLIENT_SECRET, crc_token)) }`; events `POST` with header `x-twitter-webhooks-signature-oauth2` = `sha256=` + base64 HMAC of the raw body with `X_CLIENT_SECRET`; the DM event's exact JSON shape is a named build-time check in the alerts slice.

# .feature/plan-draft/data-model.md
Skills: supabase, supabase-postgres-best-practices

## 1. Files and contracts

Files: `supabase/migrations/<utc timestamp>_product_tables.sql` (one migration, mirrored after the MCP applies it), `supabase/migrations/<utc timestamp>_seed_sources.sql` (the 150 seed rows as data, generated from `docs/source-table-seed.json`), `lib/supabase/database.types.ts` (regenerated from the live catalog after both). `migrations: yes`.

Contracts: every table, column, index, constraint, policy and RPC exactly as `shared.md` section 1 lists them; nothing else in the schema. RLS enabled on every table with the policies named there and no others. The RPCs are `security definer`, `set search_path = public`, execute revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated`. `citext` is already installed (decisions.md, "Clear the ground, completion": citext remained untouched); `gen_random_uuid()` is available. The seed maps the JSON fields `id, kind, target, name, focus, lang, description, itemsPerWeek → items_per_week, postsPerDay → posts_per_day, followers, recentTitles → recent_titles`; `foundFor` and `groups` are dropped (nothing reads them). `x_user_id` is left null (accounts fill it at first poll).

Input classes: none (no entry point).

Seams cited: section 1 whole; S1 (the ledger's unique index), S3 (the status columns), the RPCs used by S2, S8, access.

## 2. Build steps

1. `$supabase:supabase-postgres-best-practices` then `$supabase:supabase`: write the product-tables migration as SQL intent from `shared.md` section 1: tables in dependency order (`sources`, `monitors`, `monitor_sources`, `monitor_accounts`, `items`, `monitor_items`, `stories`, `story_items`, `card_versions`, `deliveries`, `dm_events`, `stripe_events`, `cost_ledger`, `run_claims`, `config`, `contact_messages`, `handle_waitlist`, `digest_items`, `followed_repos`), then indexes, then `alter table ... enable row level security` on every table, then the four SELECT-only owner policies (`monitors` for `authenticated` on `user_id = (select auth.uid())`, and the three child tables through `exists (select 1 from monitors m where m.id = monitor_id and m.user_id = (select auth.uid()))`; no write policy anywhere), then `updated_at` triggers on `sources`, `monitors`, `config`, then the six RPCs (`reserve_build`, `debit_posts`, `claim_run`, `release_run`, `claim_build`, `user_id_by_email`) with the revokes, then the `config` seed rows. Apply it with the Supabase MCP `apply_migration` (name `product_tables`), mirror the SQL to the file with the `-- Applied via the Supabase MCP server` header.
2. `$supabase:supabase`: generate the seed migration from `docs/source-table-seed.json` (a small Node one-liner run once, its output committed; no script file kept): 150 `insert into sources ... on conflict (id) do update set name = excluded.name, focus = excluded.focus, lang = excluded.lang, description = excluded.description, items_per_week = excluded.items_per_week, posts_per_day = excluded.posts_per_day, followers = excluded.followers, recent_titles = excluded.recent_titles`. Apply with `apply_migration` (name `seed_sources`), mirror to the file.
3. `$supabase:supabase`: regenerate `lib/supabase/database.types.ts` with the MCP `generate_typescript_types`, replace the file whole (it is generated), and run the MCP `get_advisors` for security; a finding on these tables is fixed in a follow-up migration `<ts>_advisor_fixes.sql` applied and mirrored the same way (an advisor finding about pre-existing auth objects is logged and left).

## 3. Acceptance journeys

- J1: In the Supabase dashboard's table editor the 19 tables exist, `sources` holds 150 rows, every table shows RLS enabled, and `select reserve_build('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::uuid, 3)` in the SQL editor (run as the service role) returns `true` once the config row `anon_budget_usd` is 200, and the ledger gains one `reservation` row.

## 4. Owner does at ship

none

# .feature/plan-draft/core.md
Skills: nextjs, vercel-functions, supabase, instrument-integration, instrument-product-analytics, instrument-error-tracking, instrument-llm-analytics, ai-gateway, shadcn

## 1. Files and contracts

Files owned: `lib/guards/ledger.ts` (S1), `lib/guards/guards.ts` (S2), `lib/guards/claims.ts` (S8), `lib/guards/watchdog.ts`, `lib/monitor-state.ts` (S3), `lib/analytics/events.ts` (S4), `lib/x/client.ts` (S5), `lib/x/handle.ts` (S22), `lib/ai/jev.ts` (S6), `lib/ai/cost.ts` (S7), `lib/observability/` (existing files; the reference-init diff), `lib/supabase/admin.ts` (adds the missing `import "server-only"`; Astra, turn 1), `app/api/cron/credits/route.ts`, `vercel.json` (crons), `package.json`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `pnpm-workspace.yaml`, `next.config.ts`, `app/layout.tsx`, `instrumentation-client.ts`, `components/ui/` (primitives added by the CLI only), `components/site-header.tsx`, `components/site-footer.tsx` (S19; new files with today's header and footer content; the old landing files stay until landing deletes them), `scripts/README.md` (one paragraph).

Contracts: S1 to S8, S19, S22 exactly as `shared.md` states. `guards()` reads `config` once per call. `reserveCost` fails closed: when the row cannot be written the paid call does not run (the error propagates and is reported); `settleCost` failures are reported and the row stays unsettled (the daily reconcile settles it at its reserved amount). The X client's `xSendDm` uses `X_BOT_BEARER_TOKEN` and `POST /2/dm_conversations/with/{recipient}/messages` with `{ text }` (proven September 28) and reads the DM event id from `data.dm_event_id`; every other call uses `X_BEARER_TOKEN`; the one-request-per-second queue from `engine.ts` (`xTurn`) moves here unchanged. `xCounts` uses `GET /2/tweets/counts/recent?query=from:{handle} -is:reply -is:retweet&granularity=day` and returns the 7-day mean. The credits cron: `GET /2/usage/credits` with the app token → `config.x_balance` (`console = data.total_balance`, `checked_at`, `status`); then `config.bot` from `GET /2/users/me` with the bot token when null; then the watchdog over every source (section 2 of shared.md); then the reconcile of unsettled ledger rows older than 10 minutes; then the pause pass (`monitors` with 14 days since the later of `build_finished_at` and `last_viewed_at` → `status = 'paused'`); emits `x_balance_read`.

Input classes: the credits route admits only `Authorization: Bearer ${CRON_SECRET}` (401 otherwise). `reserveCost` and `settleCost` validate their input with zod schemas (Check at the door).

Packages added (one step, `pnpm add`): `stripe`, `botid`, `nodemailer` and `@types/nodemailer`. shadcn primitives added (one step, `pnpm dlx shadcn add`): `card`, `tabs`, `switch`, `table`, `alert`, `skeleton`, `avatar`, `radio-group`, `alert-dialog`, `progress`, `checkbox`, `dropdown-menu`, `sheet`, `scroll-area` (existing: badge, button-group, button, collapsible, dialog, input, label, select, separator, sonner, spinner, textarea, tooltip). No primitive is hand-edited.

## 2. Build steps

1. `$vercel:nextjs`: add the packages with `pnpm add stripe botid nodemailer` and `pnpm add -D @types/nodemailer` (a named build-time check, "package exports": the import names used later come from each package's `.d.ts`, never from memory); add the primitives with one `pnpm dlx shadcn add card tabs switch table alert skeleton avatar radio-group alert-dialog progress checkbox dropdown-menu sheet scroll-area`. Create `scripts/README.md` (one paragraph: owner-run Node scripts, run with `node scripts/<name>.mjs`, they read `.env.local` and print no secret). Add `import "server-only"` as the first line of `lib/supabase/admin.ts`.
2. `$supabase:supabase`: `lib/guards/ledger.ts` (S1: `reserveCost`, `settleCost`, `withCost`, zod schemas, the `spend_recorded` event on settle); `lib/guards/claims.ts` (S8) over `claim_run`/`release_run` with `run_id = crypto.randomUUID()`; `lib/guards/guards.ts` (S2) reading `config.kill_switch`, `config.anon_budget_usd` versus the reservation sum, `config.x_balance` → `balanceEstimate = console - (x spend in cost_ledger since checked_at, unsettled rows at their reserved amount)`, a stale or unknown balance (never read, or `checked_at` older than 25 hours) triggers one `GET /2/usage/credits` on the spot and, still unknown, counts as closed; `anonBuildsOpen = !killSwitch && estimate known && estimate >= 15 && reservations + 3 <= budget`, `trialPollingOpen = !killSwitch && estimate known && estimate >= 15`.
3. `lib/monitor-state.ts` (S3) as a pure function with the table of shared.md section 3 (the `Pick` fields listed there), plus `nextDailySlot(m, now)` (the next `alert_hour` in `alert_timezone` using `Intl.DateTimeFormat` parts, no library; a DST-skipped hour moves to the next valid local time) used by alerts.
4. `$posthog:instrument-product-analytics`: `lib/analytics/events.ts` (S4): the `EventName` union from shared.md section 4, `track()` over `getPostHogServerClient()` with `distinctId` defaulting to `server`, `waitUntil`-safe, never throwing.
5. `$vercel:ai-gateway` then `$posthog:instrument-llm-analytics`: `lib/ai/cost.ts` (S7: `gatewayCost` returning `{charged, market}`, and `withCost`-wrapped helpers for the two Gateway model calls; the `cost || marketCost` fallback from `engine.ts` is not carried) and `lib/ai/jev.ts` (S6): the `jev()` from `engine.ts` generalized to any question set, the same request shape, zod-checked, batching at 60 questions, the state-size failure, one retry, reserving $0.01 per request through S1 and settling with `charged`, and one `captureAiGeneration` per request (model `typesafe-ai/jev`, stage = `opts.kind`).
6. `lib/x/client.ts` (S5): `xTurn`, `xGet`, `xPost`, `xBill` moved from `engine.ts` (the engine keeps its `toPosts`, fields and rendering), the 429 retry honoring `x-rate-limit-reset`, `xUserByHandle`, `xTimeline`, `xCounts` (null on failure), `xSendDm` with the `uncertain` flag on timeout or 5xx without a body; each reserves before the call (`maxPosts` from the caller) and settles through S1. `lib/x/handle.ts` gains `RESERVED_HANDLES` and `isReservedHandle` (S22).
7. `$vercel:vercel-functions`: `app/api/cron/credits/route.ts` (the contract above; `maxDuration = 300`; the claim `credits`, ttl 360), `lib/guards/watchdog.ts` (`checkSourceSpend(sourceId)` and `checkAllSources()`: sum `cost_ledger` by `source_id` for the current UTC day with unsettled rows at their reserved amount; pause over $1.00; `source_paused` event; the feed cron calls the first before each source, the credits cron the second); `vercel.json` gains the `crons` array of shared.md section 4 exactly (eight entries).
8. `$vercel:nextjs`: `app/layout.tsx` adds the `botid` client component (`BotIdClient` from `botid/client` with `protect` listing `POST /api/build` and `POST /api/view`; the exact export names are a named build-time check against `node_modules/botid/*.d.ts`: if the client component is named differently, use the documented name; if `protect` takes a different shape, follow the `.d.ts`), and `next.config.ts` wraps with `withBotId` from `botid/next/config` when that export exists (same check; if it does not, log the decision). `components/site-header.tsx` and `components/site-footer.tsx` are the moved header and footer (S19), importing `landingContent.brand` still from `lib/landing/content.ts` (read only), and the header's sign-in buttons keep linking to `/login` and `/signup`.
9. # .feature/plan-draft/onboarding.md
Skills: nextjs, vercel-functions, ai-sdk, ai-gateway, typesafe-ai, prompt-format, supabase

## 1. Files and contracts

Files owned: `lib/onboarding/engine.ts`, `lib/onboarding/prompts.ts`, `lib/onboarding/types.ts`, `lib/onboarding/run.ts` (new: the durable runner), `app/api/build/route.ts`, `app/api/build/retry/route.ts`, `app/api/cron/builds/route.ts`; deleted: `app/onboarding/page.tsx`, `app/onboarding/onboarding-client.tsx`, `app/api/onboarding/route.ts` (the bare page and its stream; the landing box replaces them; owner, September 28: he builds from the blank page like anyone).

Contracts:
- `POST /api/build` (S9): form fields `handle` (validated by `normalizeValidHandle`, refused when `isReservedHandle` → `303 /?error=handle`), `beat` (1 to 300 characters, trimmed → `303 /?error=beat`). Order: kill switch and `guards()` → `build_refused` redirect `303 /?closed=1` when `anonBuildsOpen` is false; bot check (`checkBotId()` from `botid/server`; a bot gets 403); existing `monitors` row for the handle → `303 /<handle>` (one build per handle, owner; existing pages open even when admission is closed); else `reserve_build(newMonitorId, 3)` → false: `303 /?closed=1`; true: insert `monitors` (`status = 'building'`, `built_by = session user id or null`, `build_started_at`), take `claim_build`, run step 1 (the X lookup, about a second) inside the request: not found → the row is set `failed` with `build_error = 'Handle not found'`, the reservation released (a `reservation` row with negative usd and `kind = 'release'`), and `303 /?error=notfound&handle=<handle>` so the error shows under the box (owner, September 27); X failing → `failed`, `'The X lookup could not finish. Try again.'`, `303 /?error=lookup&handle=`; found → `track('agent_build_started')`, `after(runBuild(monitorId))`, `303 /<handle>`. `maxDuration = 800`. Every build without a paying owner, the owner's own included, draws on the $200 total (no special seats; owner, September 28).
- `POST /api/build/retry` json `{ monitorId }`: only when `status = 'failed'` and `build_tries < 2`; takes `claim_build` and restarts `runBuild` from the saved checkpoint (no new reservation; the existing one stays; owner plan: only the extra work is charged); `303 /<handle>`; at two tries the page shows the failure without the button (owner: two tries then failed).
- `GET /api/cron/builds` (every 5 minutes, S8 claim `builds`): resumes every `building` monitor whose `build_lease_until` has expired by taking `claim_build` and calling `runBuild` (Astra, turn 1: a closed tab or a killed function must not strand a build); a monitor that cannot be claimed (two tries used) is set `failed` with `build_error = 'Building stopped: too many tries.'`.
- `runBuild(monitorId)` in `run.ts`: reads the row, renews `build_lease_until` (10 minutes) at each step, runs the engine's steps with `report(step, message)` appending to `build_log` and setting `build_step`; each step saves its checkpoint in `build_state` (`profile` and `x_user_id` after step 1, `posts` after step 2, `scores` after Jev, `answer` and `searched` after each model call) so a resume never repeats a paid call that finished; step 3 writes S10 rows, `brief`, `status = 'live'`, `build_finished_at`, `track('agent_built', { cost_usd })`; any error: `status = 'failed'`, `build_error = <step>: <message>`, `track('agent_build_failed')`, `reportServerException`.
- The engine: `runOnboarding` keeps its three steps, its prompt and its Jev question; changes: (a) the X calls go through `lib/x/client.ts` (S5) and the in-engine `xTurn`, `xGet`, `xBill`, `costOf` and the `jev()` function are deleted in favor of S5, S6, S7; (b) `WALKTHROUGH_STEPS` and the `writer` parameter are removed; the engine takes `{ handle, beat, report, checkpoint: (state) => Promise<void>, resume?: BuildState }` and returns `{ profile, posts, final, brief, costUsd, turns }`, skipping every step whose result is in `resume`; (c) the profile request is trimmed to `description,entities,id,name,pinned_tweet_id,profile_image_url,url,username` and the `affiliation.user_id` expansion is dropped (owner, September 28); (d) the model's structured answer gains `brief: { summary: string; interests: string[]; languages: string[]; topic_terms: string[] }` (a detailed brief on the person: who they are, what they follow and publish, in which languages, and 3 to 8 topic terms the digests search with; `summary` at most 1,200 characters, the assistant's cap) written in the same call as the picks (the three visible steps stay three), and `prompts.ts` gains one `<brief>` section describing it (prompt-format rules: sections, placeholders for numbers, no digits typed by hand); (e) the Jev question and lines stay as they are (`POSSIBLE = 0.35`, owner); (f) the Gateway calls go through S7's `withCost` helpers (reserve $0.50, settle with `charged`).
- Input classes: the form (validated, rejected with `303 /?error=handle` or `/?error=beat`); the retry json (zod, 400 otherwise); the engine's X responses (already schema-checked through `toPosts`, kept).

Seams cited: S1, S2, S5, S6, S7, S9, S10, S22.

## 2. Build steps

1. `$vercel:ai-sdk` then `$typesafe-ai` then `$prompt-format`: in `engine.ts` remove `WALKTHROUGH_STEPS`, the writer, the in-engine X queue, `xGet`, `xBill`, `costOf` and `jev`; import S5, S6, S7; trim `USER_FIELDS` and the affiliation expansion; add `brief` to the `Answer` schema and the `<brief>` section to `prompts.ts` with `{BRIEF_CHARS}` filled from a constant (1200); return the result object; `report()` calls where `writer.write(data-status)` were.
2. `$supabase:supabase`: `lib/onboarding/run.ts`: `runBuild` as above, writing S10 rows with the admin client (`monitor_sources` from `final.sites`, `monitor_accounts` from `final.accounts` with `watched = true`, `sources` upserts for non-seed accounts), the checkpoint and resume logic over `monitors.build_state`, the lease renewals, and the PostHog AI generation capture (`captureAiGeneration`, stage `onboarding`).
3. `$vercel:vercel-functions` then `$vercel:nextjs`: `app/api/build/route.ts`, `app/api/build/retry/route.ts` and `app/api/cron/builds/route.ts` as the contracts above (`after()` from `next/server`; `maxDuration = 800` on the build route, 300 on the cron; the bot check's import is a named build-time check against `node_modules/botid/*.d.ts`, same as core step 8).
4. Delete `app/onboarding/` and `app/api/onboarding/`; delete `PageProfile`, `PageMedia`, `OnboardingData`, `OnboardingUIMessage` from `types.ts` (dead once the stream is gone; the page reads `monitors.profile` instead); keep `Final`, `HANDLE_NOT_FOUND`.

## 3. Acceptance journeys

- J4: On localhost, signed out, submit `ottleyai` and `AI developments and practical tools` in the landing box; the browser lands on `/ottleyai` building; within about a minute the page shows the profile, the brief, 9 or so sites and at least 5 accounts (the run cost about $0.28 on September 27); the ledger holds a $3 reservation row, one `x` row per post and profile, one `gateway` row, one `jev` row.
- J5: Submit `ottleyai` again: the browser lands on the existing page and no second `monitors` row exists.
- J6: Submit a handle that does not exist on X (`zzqqxx_none_9`): the landing box shows `Handle not found` under the handle field, no page is created, and the ledger shows the reservation and its release. Then, while a build is running, close the tab and reopen the page: it shows the same progress and no second `monitors` row or reservation exists.

## 4. Owner does at ship

# .feature/plan-draft/collect.md
Skills: nextjs, vercel-functions, supabase

## 1. Files and contracts

Files owned: `lib/sources/discovery.ts`, `lib/sources/feed.ts`, `lib/sources/sitemap.ts`, `lib/sources/article-text.ts`, `lib/http-fetch.ts`, `lib/xml.ts` (existing, edited), `lib/collect/poll.ts`, `lib/collect/listing.ts`, `lib/collect/date.ts`, `lib/collect/item.ts`, `app/api/cron/collect/route.ts`.

Contracts:
- The cron (`collect`, every minute, S8 claim, `guards()` kill switch): selects sources of kind `rss` or `website` with `paused_at is null and next_fetch_at <= now()` that at least one live monitor watches (join `monitor_sources ... removed_at is null` and `monitors.status in ('live')` with `monitorState` in dormant, trial or paid), up to 60 per run (assistant's number), fetched in parallel (8 at once, the lab's number). Each source: a conditional request (`If-None-Match` from `etag`, `If-Modified-Since` from `last_modified`) through `fetchSafeSourceWithFinalUrl` with the user agent changed to a normal Chrome browser string (owner, September 19; `discovery.ts` today sends `Oparax source discovery`); 304 → only `last_fetched_at`, `next_fetch_at = now() + 1 minute`; 200 → entries per R26 (feeds: `pubDate`/`date`/`published` before `updated`; RDF handled; the entry's image from enclosure, media thumbnail or first body image) or R27 (listing links: same site, not navigation paths, article-shaped last segment of at least 8 characters with a hyphen or digit, no file extensions; `discovery.ts`'s query-parameter stripping is limited to the R1 tracking parameters). New candidates (not in `items` by R1 id) have their article page fetched (8 at once per source), dated per R28, titled per R29, read per R30 (the reader in `article-text.ts` is brought to R30: the article or main element first, the 400-character paragraph rule, the 60 percent body rule, quoted passages marked `[quote] ... [end quote]`, denials kept, the title as the first line), given an outcome per R3 (`full` at 400+ characters, `short` at 120 to 399, else `unreadable` with reason `under_120`, `teaser`, `unavailable`, `furniture`), and inserted into `items` (`kind = 'article'`). Undated pages are left out (R26, R28). Source health: `unreadable_streak` counts consecutive unreadable items; a readable one resets it. `next_fetch_at = now() + 1 minute` (owner, September 28). A fetch error: `reportServerException`, `next_fetch_at = now() + 5 minutes`, no state change otherwise.
- Day zero (R25, owner): for every `monitor_sources` row with `prefilled_at is null` on a monitor in dormant, trial or paid state, the run fetches that source regardless of `next_fetch_at` (once per source per run even when several monitors need it), takes the union of the cached `items` for that source and the fresh entries, keeps only those published within the last 2 days, attaches at most the 10 newest to that monitor (`monitor_items pending`), then sets `prefilled_at = now()`; a source that fails to fetch keeps `prefilled_at` null and is retried next run (Astra, turn 1). Sources of kind `x_account` are never fetched here (accounts owns them).
- Every new `items` row triggers, in the same run, `monitor_items (status = 'pending')` for every monitor watching that source (S11); an item seen again through a second source appends that source to `items.source_ids`.
- Input classes: the cron header (401 otherwise); every fetched body passes the existing size guards (5 MB) and the SSRF checks; XML and HTML are parsed defensively (a parse failure is `unreadable: unavailable`).

Seams cited: S1 (no paid calls here; fetching is free), S2, S3, S8, S11.

## 2. Build steps

1. `$vercel:nextjs`: `lib/sources/discovery.ts`: the user agent becomes a current Chrome desktop string (owner, September 19); `fetchSafeSourceWithFinalUrl` gains optional `headers` (for the conditional request) and returns `etag`/`last-modified` from the response; the query-parameter stripping follows R1's tracking list only.
2. `lib/sources/feed.ts`: R26 date precedence, RDF (`rdf:RDF` with `item` children) parsed, entry image per shared.md's `items.image` rule; `lib/sources/article-text.ts`: the R30 reader (quotes marked, denials kept, the 400/60 percent/200 rules, title first) with `via` kept for records.
3. `lib/collect/date.ts` (R28), `lib/collect/item.ts` (R1 id, R29 title, R3 outcome), `lib/collect/listing.ts` (R27), `lib/collect/poll.ts` (the per-source flow above, parallel with a concurrency limit of 8 written inline with `Promise.all` over chunks, no library).
4. `$vercel:vercel-functions`: `app/api/cron/collect/route.ts`: `maxDuration = 300`, the claim, the guard, the source selection with day-zero first, `run_completed` with counts (`sources`, `items`, `unreadable`).

## 3. Acceptance journeys

- J7: With the `ottleyai` agent built (J4) and `CRON_SECRET` in `.env.local`, call `curl -H "Authorization: Bearer $CRON_SECRET" localhost:3000/api/cron/collect`; the `items` table gains up to 10 items per source from the last 2 days, each with full text (over 400 characters for most), every `monitor_sources` row has `prefilled_at` set; `monitor_items` holds one pending row per item.
- J8: Call it again a minute later: sources answer 304 or return nothing new; `run_completed` shows `items: 0`; a feed source with a new article (check TechCrunch's feed) shows the new item within the next call.

## 4. Owner does at ship

none

# .feature/plan-draft/feed.md
Skills: ai-sdk, ai-gateway, typesafe-ai, prompt-format, instrument-llm-analytics, supabase

## 1. Files and contracts

Files owned: `lib/feed/fit.ts`, `lib/feed/group.ts`, `lib/feed/adds.ts`, `lib/feed/writer.ts`, `lib/feed/prompts.ts`, `lib/feed/checks.ts`, `lib/feed/repair.ts`, `lib/feed/process.ts`, `lib/feed/types.ts`, `app/api/cron/feed/route.ts`.

Contracts (the specification is `docs/downstream-algorithm.md`, its R-rules cited by number; every number here is a code constant, filled into prompts through placeholders per `prompt-format`):
- The cron (`feed`, every minute, S8 claim, `guards()` kill switch): selects monitors in dormant, trial or paid state (S3) with pending `monitor_items`; processes monitors in parallel (8 at once), items within one monitor one at a time in `published_at` order with the item id as the tie-break (R13); before an item is processed, `checkSourceSpend(item.source_id)` (core's watchdog) runs and a paused source's items stay pending; at most 40 items per monitor per run (assistant's number) and no new item admitted after 240 seconds, so a run stays inside `maxDuration = 300`; the rest wait for the next minute.
- Per item: `items.outcome = 'unreadable'` → `monitor_items.status = 'skipped'`, `fit_band = null`, no call. Else fit (R6 question verbatim, one Jev request per item through S6, `kind = 'fit'`) over the state `{ beat, person: { brief: the jsonb brief }, source: { name, focus, description, why }, preferences: [], examples: [], item: { title, text, published_at } }` (owner, September 28: the brief and the source's why go to Jev; preferences and examples stay empty this build; the state-size failure from S6 leaves the item `failed` with `error = 'state too large'`, never truncated); lines R7/R8 (`FIT_ON = 0.5`, `FIT_OFF = 0.35`, unsure counts off); a source with `monitor_sources.no_filter = true` is `on` without a call. Off or unsure → `status = 'skipped'` with the score. On → `status = 'on'`, then the single-source card (S16) and grouping.
- Single-source card (owner, September 22: both views): one writer call per fitting item (R15 to R20 with the item alone), checks (R17 code, R18 support and attribution, R19 headline, at `SUPPORT_LINE = 0.5`), one repair (R31), the headline that ships (R32); the verified card in S12 shape written to `monitor_items.card`, `card_status`.
- Grouping (articles and posts alike; owner, September 28): open stories = this monitor's stories with `last_published_at` within `WINDOW_H = 72` hours either way of the item (R10), batched at 60 per Jev request; question R11 verbatim per story (`kind = 'group'`); the item joins every story at `JOIN_LINE = 0.75` or above (assistant's default, recorded); none → a new story (`status = 'open'`, `fallback_title = item title`, `opened_at`, `last_published_at`, `story_items role = 'opened'`) and the story card is written (the same writer path with `<previous_card>` absent). Joined → the adds question R14 (`kind = 'adds'`, `ADDS_LINE = 0.75`): at or above → rewrite the story card (R15 with `<previous_card>`), below → `story_items role = 'attached'`, nothing rewritten. A grouping call failure → `status = 'pending'` with `error = 'grouping failed'` and `tries + 1`; adds failure → same; two tries → `status = 'failed'`.
- Writer: model `alibaba/qwen3.7-flash`, `reasoning: 'medium'` (the lab's accepted option; the exact AI SDK option name is confirmed against `node_modules/ai/docs` at build, a named check), temperature 0, `maxOutputTokens = 6000`, a 240-second abort; the system prompt of section 7 verbatim with `{FACTS_MIN}=1`, `{FACTS_MAX}=5`, `{EVIDENCE_MIN}=1`, `{EVIDENCE_MAX}=3` filled from constants; the user message per R15 with `<beat>`, `<person>` (the brief's summary and interests; owner, September 28), `<source why="...">` (the source's why sentence; owner), `<previous_card>`, `<item>`; posts render as `<item publisher="@handle (name)">`; a post's own text is body evidence (the title-only rule applies to articles only). Output validated by zod per R20 with one retry carrying `<retry>`. Every call through S7's `withCost` (reserve $0.02, settle with `charged`; `kind = 'write'|'repair'`) and captured with `captureAiGeneration` (stage `write`/`repair`, `distinctId = monitor id`).
- Story card save: `stories.version` check (`update ... where version = :v`), `card_versions` row with the full record (R9 section 9 fields), `last_changed_at` on a new or changed fact (R22), `stories.status` in `written|no_card|write_failed`, `image` from the first item with one.
- Watched posts as items: `items.kind = 'post'` rows from accounts go through the same path; `source` for Jev is the account row (`name`, `focus = ''`, `description = bio if known`), and `why` from `monitor_accounts.why`.
- Input classes: the cron header; model output through zod (R20); Jev output through S6's schema.

Seams cited: S1, S2, S3, S6, S7, S8, S11, S12, S16.

## 2. Build steps

1. `$prompt-format` then `$typesafe-ai`: `lib/feed/prompts.ts` with the writer system prompt (section 7 verbatim, placeholders), the retry and repair sentences, the four Jev questions (R6, R11, R14, R18 support and attribution, R19) as constants; `lib/feed/types.ts` (the card, the write record, the item view).
2. `$vercel:ai-sdk` then `$vercel:ai-gateway` then `$posthog:instrument-llm-analytics`: `lib/feed/writer.ts` (`generateText` with `Output.object`, the reasoning option check, the retry, ledger and capture), `lib/feed/checks.ts` (R17 normalization and span search, numbers rule, title-only rule; R18 and R19 through S6), `lib/feed/repair.ts` (R31, R32).
3. `lib/feed/fit.ts`, `lib/feed/group.ts`, `lib/feed/adds.ts` through S6 with the batching and the lines as constants.
4. `$supabase:supabase`: `lib/feed/process.ts`: the per-monitor serial loop, the single-source card, grouping, the versioned saves, `card_versions`, the pending and failed states with `tries`.
5. `$vercel:vercel-functions`: `app/api/cron/feed/route.ts` (`maxDuration = 300`, claim, guards, parallel monitors, `run_completed` with counts `judged`, `on`, `skipped`, `stories`, `cards`, `failed`).

## 3. Acceptance journeys

- J9: After J7, call `curl -H "Authorization: Bearer $CRON_SECRET" localhost:3000/api/cron/feed` (repeat until `run_completed` shows nothing pending); `monitor_items` shows fit scores with `on`/`skipped`, every `on` item has a `card` with 1 to 5 facts whose spans are found verbatim in the item text, and `stories` holds cards; on September 27 Liam's run gave 23 items, 23 on, 21 stories, 81 facts.
- J10: Two items reporting one event (find one in the run, or add TechCrunch and The Verge on a shared launch) sit in one story with two publishers and `story_items` roles `opened` and `joined` or `attached`.
- J11: A source marked `no_filter` in `monitor_sources` (set by hand in the table editor) has every item `on` with `fit_score` null.

## 4. Owner does at ship

# .feature/plan-draft/page.md
Skills: nextjs, react-best-practices, shadcn, frontend-design, web-design-guidelines, accessibility, beautiful-shadows, ai-elements, emil-design-eng, instrument-product-analytics

## 1. Files and contracts

Files owned: `app/[handle]/page.tsx`, `app/[handle]/[story]/page.tsx`, `app/[handle]/not-found.tsx`, `app/api/view/route.ts`, `app/api/activation/route.ts` (S15), `components/monitor/agent-header.tsx`, `components/monitor/building.tsx`, `components/monitor/sources-list.tsx`, `components/monitor/accounts-strip.tsx`, `components/monitor/feed.tsx`, `components/monitor/story-card.tsx` (S21), `components/monitor/item-card.tsx`, `components/monitor/skipped-list.tsx`, `components/monitor/digest-block.tsx`, `components/monitor/state-banner.tsx`, `components/monitor/pay-buttons.tsx`, `components/monitor/bot-button.tsx`, `components/monitor/view-beacon.tsx`, `components/monitor/refresh-while-building.tsx`, `lib/monitor/content.ts` (every string of this surface not in shared.md section 5), `lib/monitor/read.ts` (the server reads).

Screens, one line each (stock shadcn Mira per DESIGN.md; the ui skills' planning decisions follow):
- The building state: the agent header with the handle, then an `ai-elements` `ChainOfThought` of the four steps from `build_log` (`ChainOfThoughtStep` with `status` active/complete, the profile step showing the picture and bio as the old page did), refreshed by `refresh-while-building.tsx` (`router.refresh()` every 3 seconds while `status = 'building'`, no websocket); motion: only the step shimmer (`emil-design-eng`: motion answers a live step). Failed: an `Alert` (destructive) with the copy and a `Try again` button posting to `/api/build/retry`.
- The page: header (`SiteHeader`, S19), `agent-header` (avatar `Avatar` 48 px, name, `@handle` in mono, the beat, the brief in a `Card`), `state-banner` (trial line, frozen banner with `pay-buttons` as three `Card`s each with a `Button`, lapsed banner, paused line, pool line; semantic colors: amber for days left under 3, red for frozen), `bot-button` (a `Button` linking to X, or the connected line), `Tabs` with `Stories` and `Articles` (the two views; the tab row is a real `Tabs`, keyboard reachable), the feed (`StoryCard` and `ItemCard` as `Card`s with `beautiful-shadows` medium, headline in Nunito Sans, facts as a list with the publisher name and a link per fact's source, `{n} further reports` as a `Collapsible`, the time in mono, an image at the top when present, posts with the account's handle and name), the skipped list (a `Collapsible` "Skipped" with score badges), `sources-list` (each source: name, focus, the why sentence, health line), `accounts-strip` (handles as `Badge`s, `watched` ones marked), `digest-block` (when switches are on), `SiteFooter`. Touch targets 44 px under 700 px (`accessibility`); AA contrast from the tokens; every icon-only control labelled; `desk:` for the two-column layout above 700 px (feed left, sources and accounts right, `grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_360px]`).
- The story link `/[handle]/[story]`: the same page with that story's card first and marked (`aria-current`), the tab set to Stories, and `scroll-margin-top` for the sticky header; 404 for an unknown story.
- Not found: `/[handle]` with no agent → `not-found.tsx` with the line `No agent for @{handle} yet.` and a link to `/`.

Contracts:
- Reads (`lib/monitor/read.ts`, admin client, server components only; nothing per-user leaves the server): the monitor row minus `stripe_*`, `bot_code`, `posts`, `build_log` (the building state reads `build_log` separately); `monitor_sources` joined to `sources` (health from `unreadable_streak`); `monitor_accounts`; the newest 60 stories with cards (`status = 'written'` or `no_card` with `fallback_title`), ordered by `last_changed_at desc`; the newest 60 `monitor_items` with cards for the Articles view, ordered by `published_at desc`; skipped items with scores (newest 30); `digest_items` (newest 30); the caller's session (for the owner exclusion and the settings link when `user_id` matches).
- The view beacon (S13): `view-beacon.tsx` POSTs `/api/view` once after mount (not on the owner's session: the server passes `isOwner` so the beacon is not rendered for him); the route checks `checkBotId()`, the session email against `OWNER_EMAILS`, then updates `trial_started_at` (once, when `status = 'live'` and null), `last_viewed_at`, `alert_timezone` from `x-vercel-ip-timezone` when null, and restarts a paused monitor (`status = 'live'`); emits `trial_started` and `page_viewed_outside` on the first view only; json `{ok}`.
- Pay buttons (S14): three forms posting to `/api/stripe/checkout` with `monitorId` and `tier`; `paywall_shown` captured in the browser once when the frozen banner mounts.
- Bot button (S15): renders when `bot_state` is `none` or `stopped` and the state is trial or paid; it is a form posting `monitorId` to `POST /api/activation`, which writes the code and expiry and redirects to the X composer (never a mutation on GET; Astra, turn 1); the paused and active states show their lines from shared.md section 5.
- Input classes: `handle` param validated (`normalizeValidHandle`, reserved → 404); `story` param must be a uuid; the beacon json `{monitorId: uuid}` (400 otherwise).

Seams cited: S2, S3, S12, S13, S14, S15, S16, S17, S19, S21, S22.

## 2. Build steps

3. `$ai-elements` then `$emil-design-eng`: `building.tsx` (ChainOfThought), `refresh-while-building.tsx`, `state-banner.tsx`, `pay-buttons.tsx`, `bot-button.tsx`, `view-beacon.tsx`, `agent-header.tsx`.
4. `$vercel:nextjs` then `$web-design-guidelines`: `app/[handle]/page.tsx` (server component, awaited `params`, `generateMetadata` with the agent's name and beat, the two-column layout, the tabs, the banner by `monitorState`; the page renders for every state, including when new-build admission is closed), `app/[handle]/[story]/page.tsx` (reuses the page component with `storyId`, scoped to that handle), `app/[handle]/not-found.tsx`, `app/api/view/route.ts` (S13), `app/api/activation/route.ts` (S15), `posthog` browser captures (`paywall_shown`).

## 3. Acceptance journeys

- J13: The same visit set `trial_started_at` (table editor) and the page shows `7 days left in your free week. Plans from $5 a month.`; reload as the owner (signed in with an `OWNER_EMAILS` address) before the first visit on a fresh agent: no `trial_started_at` is set.
- J14: Open `localhost:3000/ottleyai/<a story id>`: the page opens with that card first and marked; an unknown id gives the not-found page.
- J15: Set `trial_started_at` to 8 days ago in the table editor and reload: the frozen banner with the three prices and buttons shows, the feed is the old cards, `paywall_shown` appears in PostHog's live events.

Localhost cannot prove the bot check or the timezone header.
